    casa-rs and CASA 6.7.6.14 tasks and tools¶

    Truth class: generated reference Generated by: scripts/check-casa-inventory.py --write Sources: resources/casa-compat/casa-6.7.6.14/ (read from CASA by tools/casa-compat/dump_casa_task_inventory.py), crates/casa-provider-contracts/resources/parameter-surfaces.json, parameter-catalog.json, the CASA class signatures in crates/casars-python/python/tests/data/, and reviewed resources/casa-compat/semantics/

    Do not edit this file by hand. Regenerate it after changing the inventory, a surface, a recorded class signature or a reviewed file.

    Each casa-rs task that mimics a CASA task (#931) against that task as CASA 6.7.6.14 defines it: whether it presents CASA's signature, the CASA parameters casa-rs does not take yet, those it takes but accepts only at CASA's default, the parameters casa-rs takes that CASA does not (casa-rs names for CASA concepts, or casa-rs-only controls), whether the shared parameters keep CASA's order, and the defaults that differ. calibrate, msconvert and msexplore are casa-rs tasks with no CASA counterpart.

    The CASA parameters casa-rs lacks or accepts only at their defaults are tracked by family: images in #977, MeasurementSet and flagging in #978, calibration in #979 and tclean in #980.

    For direct cBool, cFloat, cInt, cPath and cStr schemas with the recorded coercions, a value both implementations reject for type reports CASA's wording, unless a narrower unimplemented-form diagnostic applies. Direct cBoolVec, cFloatVec, cIntVec, cPathVec and cStrVec schemas are also covered where the recorded vector coercions apply. Float-shaped cIntVec inputs keep the native diagnostic because a JSON integer outside the native range may lose its original spelling. For explicit scalar inputs checked by the task, a missing absolute or explicitly relative path gets CASA's wording when the schema has one direct required path. Other required-path cases, unions and variants retain casa-rs's diagnostics (#1099). CASA accepts booleans as integers and coerces them to floats; where casa-rs refuses one, it keeps its own message. Exception classes follow the casa-rs Python contract.

    simobserve parameter implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing forms or combinations; Default only is derived from the runtime refusal contract; Not implemented has no implementation; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below. No signature or return-structure result establishes scientific equivalence.

    Audit baseline: 49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947. Status counts: Default only: 0, Implemented: 37, Not implemented: 0, Partial: 1, Unassessed: 0.

    Mappings, defaults, catalog domains, constraints and conditional default-only refusals are generated from the existing task contract. The catalog may admit values that the native reader then refuses: the reviewed scope and linked combinations describe those restrictions.

    simobserve interacting modes and task behavior¶

    Sky model and component list¶

    Partial — skymodel through modifymodel and flatimage with inbright, indirection, incell, incenter and inwidth; complist with compwidth and comp_nchan, alone or with a sky model.

    Paired task evidence now covers scalar indirection and incell overrides, separately and together, and a four-channel component-only model. Additional combinations (such as overrides with combined image/component input and multi-channel image models) remain outside this receipt; the behavior remains Partial.

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, model-overrides-20261111, main-integration-20261111.

    Antenna lists¶

    Partial — antennalist and sdantlist as files, ALMA resolution strings ('alma;0.5arcsec'), the search through the project directory and CASA's alma/simmos, and sdant.

    Configuration names need CASA's data directory: casa-rs does not ship alma/simmos (an open owner question on #1053).

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, uvw-long-baselines-20261112.

    Pointings¶

    Partial — setpointings with direction, mapsize, maptype and pointingspacing, or a ptgfile; written to .ptg.txt and checked against the model.

    CASA task evidence includes square interferometric pointings, a three-entry user pointing file with 10/20/30-second dwell times over two full passes, and a 16 by 12 arcsecond hexagonal map with explicit 4 arcsecond spacing. Other file formats and pointing/model/mode combinations remain outside these receipts.

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, scheduling-20261111, pointings-20261111, main-integration-20261111.

    Calibrator and observing schedule¶

    Partial — caldirection and calflux, integration, totaltime, refdate and hourangle.

    Paired task evidence now covers numeric and quantity non-transit hour angles, reference dates in 2015/2016/2017/2025 and discarded time suffixes. The historical calibrator cases still use CASA sm re-prediction because the CASA task leaves calibrator scans unpredicted (approved decision); these four new cases have no calibrator.

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, scheduling-20261111, main-integration-20261111, uvw-long-baselines-20261112.

    Observation and prediction¶

    Partial — obsmode 'int', 'sd' and '' (corrupting an existing MeasurementSet), outframe, and the prediction of the model and calibrator.

    Image-model interferometry has scoped CASA comparisons for LSRK, TOPO, BARY, LSRD, GEO, GALACTO, LGROUP and CMB. REST and Undefined references and combinations with single dish, component models and existing-MS corruption remain outside these frame receipts.

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, frequency-frames-20261106, additional-frames-20261111, main-integration-20261111, uvw-long-baselines-20261112.

    Thermal noise and leakage¶

    Partial — thermalnoise 'tsys-atm', 'tsys-manual' and '' with user_pwv, t_ground, t_sky, tau0, seed, leakage and correlator.

    Noise is compared statistically (variances and KS tests), since draws differ; each environment parameter is exercised once; seed is casa-rs's own stream. CASA's own suite holds its noise tests to CASA's seed-11111 draws within 5%, which casa-rs's random stream cannot reproduce; over 20 seeds the two implementations' noise levels agree to 0.5%.

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, casa-suite-20261103, noise-seeds-20261103.

    Files, overwrite and graphics¶

    Partial — CASA's file names under the project, overwrite, verbose and graphics.

    PNG rendering (.skymodel.png and .observe.png) remains unfinished and warns (#1025). Numerical interferometric .quick.psf is implemented; broader graphics and task acceptance remain open (#1053).

    Evidence: task-tests, parity-20261103, casa-suite-20261103, quick-psf-20261111, quick-psf-relative-project-20261111.

    simobserve deviations and unfinished work¶

    compwidth-unset-spectral-increment: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: An unset compwidth gives the component-only model a meaningless spectral increment
    • Inputs: simobserve with a component list, no sky image and compwidth=''.
    • CASA: It observes 2 GHz when compwidth is not a quantity, but sets the .compskymodel spectral increment from compwidth anyway, yielding a meaningless value (the RA in radians read as hertz, 4.976 Hz for RA 19h, in CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Uses the 2 GHz simobserve observes with.
    • Rationale: The increment is the model's only spectral information beyond its reference frequency.
    • User impact: The component-only model carries a consistent spectral increment.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    calibrator-last-pass-scans-lists: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve's calibrator: the last pass, its scans and two component lists
    • Inputs: simobserve with a phase calibrator (caldirection and calflux), also with a complist or totaltime ending inside a pass.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6 never observes the pointings of an incomplete last pass (a 300 s total on 105 pointings has only the calibrator's scan), selects fields range(0,nfld) so the calibrator's scans keep zeros plus noise, and joins component lists into one nonexistent table so predict returns False.
    • casa-rs: Observes the pointings the time allows, predicts every scan including the calibrator's, and predicts both lists as one list of their components.
    • Rationale: The task means to observe and predict all of these.
    • User impact: CASA data can lack pointings, have unpredicted calibrator scans, or be all zero; casa-rs data are complete (parity cases agree at 5e-9 NRMS).
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    noisier-t-cal-left: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve leaves <stem>.noisier.T.cal
    • Inputs: simobserve with tsys-atm noise set after leakage (the noisier measurement set).
    • CASA: The setnoise table is written beside the noisy MeasurementSet, and the clean-up deletes .noisy.T.cal and .noisy.sd.T.cal but not .noisier.T.cal.
    • casa-rs: Writes no Tsys table for any of the three.
    • Rationale: The table is a by-product the task means to remove.
    • User impact: CASA leaves a stray table in the project that casa-rs never creates.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    observing-loop-never-ends: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve's observing loop never ends for pointings of no length
    • Inputs: simobserve with integration='0s' or a pointing file of zero times.
    • CASA: The while (sttime-scanstart) < totalsec loop advances by each pointing's scan length; when a pass's lengths sum to zero the time never advances and CASA loops forever, collecting pointings.
    • casa-rs: Refuses such a schedule with a message.
    • Rationale: A schedule with no length cannot be observed.
    • User impact: The task stops with an error instead of hanging.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    skymodel-deleted-when-output: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve deletes a sky model that is the model it writes
    • Inputs: simobserve with skymodel given as the full path /.skymodel (e.g. a rerun on an earlier run's model) and overwrite=True.
    • CASA: The output is the input: CASA deletes the model and then fails to read it.
    • casa-rs: Refuses the input, and the model is left in place.
    • Rationale: The user's input must not be destroyed.
    • User impact: The model survives and the user gets a clear refusal.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    project-path-nests-in-itself: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve nests a project path inside itself
    • Inputs: simobserve with project given as a path, e.g. 'a/b'.
    • CASA: The task names files fileroot + '/' + project + '.' + cfg, so 'a/b' gives a/b/a/b. in a directory that does not exist; only a plain name in the working directory works.
    • casa-rs: Names files by the directory's last component, a/b/b..
    • Rationale: For a plain name the two are the same.
    • User impact: Project paths work in casa-rs and fail in CASA.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    symlink-in-project-write-through: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve writes through a symbolic link in the project
    • Inputs: simobserve overwrite=True in a project containing a symbolic link such as .ptg.txt.
    • CASA: CASA copies ptgfile through the link, replacing the file the link points to outside the project, and creates outside files for dangling links; links to tables stop the task via rmtree; existence checks follow links.
    • casa-rs: Replaces a link in the project with the new file or table, leaves the target alone, and counts a link as existing under overwrite=False.
    • Rationale: Owner's decision of 2026-10-26: only the project's own files are replaced.
    • User impact: Files outside the project are no longer overwritten or created through links.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-26: the project's own files are the only ones overwrite replaces (recorded with the provider contracts' overwrite exception).
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    direction-list-of-one-mean: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simobserve rounds a direction list of one to its mean
    • Inputs: simobserve with direction=['J2000 18h00m00.123456789s ...'] (a list of one) versus the same direction as a string.
    • CASA: CASA passes the list to calc_pointings2, and direction_splitter centres it at its mean written at precision 5, so the map is laid out about 1e-5 s of RA (0.15 mas) from the string case.
    • casa-rs: casa-rs's simutil follows CASA, but simobserve treats a list of one like a string and lays the map out at the exact direction.
    • Rationale: The task contract resolves a string to a list of one, and the rounding carries no information.
    • User impact: A map is placed at the exact direction, differing from CASA by about 0.15 mas for a list of one.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.

    casa-suite-20261103¶

    CASA's test_task_simobserve with casa-rs in place of CASA's task — measured. Of the 54 cases CASA 6.7.6.14 passes on this host, casa-rs passes 45. Of the other 9, 3 refuse as CASA does in casa-rs's own words: an empty project, and comp_nchan and sdant of the wrong type. The other 6 are noise levels just outside the suite's 5% band: tsys-manual interferometer noise 5.8% below CASA's recorded value, and tsys-atm single-dish noise 5.05% above it in all five variants. Those six are CASA's own seed-11111 draws, which casa-rs's random stream cannot reproduce; over 20 seeds the levels agree to 0.5% (noise-seeds-20261103). 23 cases fail with CASA's own task too and 16 are skipped by both. CASA's own simobserve suite (93 cases): sky models, component lists, both, noise, bad inputs and correlators, judged by the suite's checks against casatestdata's reference products.

    • tools/simulation/simobserve_casa_suite.py

    noise-seeds-20261103¶

    CASA-paired noise levels over 20 seeds — passed. The suite's two noise cases, 20 seeds each: casa-rs's mean level over CASA's is 1.002 for testNZ_sdAtm (tsys-atm single dish) and 1.005 for testNZ_intMan (tsys-manual interferometer), within 0.2 and 0.9 standard errors. The levels CASA's suite records are CASA's own seed-11111 draws, 1.8 and 2.9 of CASA's standard deviations from its mean, which a different random stream cannot reproduce: they explain the suite's six noise failures. testNZ_sdAtm and testNZ_intMan of CASA's test_task_simobserve, the suite's noise level (noisy minus original, real part, standard deviation) per run.

    tclean parameter implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing forms or combinations; Default only is derived from the runtime refusal contract; Not implemented has no implementation; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below. No signature or return-structure result establishes scientific equivalence.

    Audit baseline: c64f13cb09054b080fe8b228a5f2c02c99bfd486. Status counts: Default only: 4, Implemented: 46, Not implemented: 0, Partial: 41, Unassessed: 1.

    Mappings, defaults, catalog domains, constraints and conditional default-only refusals are generated from the existing task contract. The catalog may admit values that the native reader then refuses: the reviewed scope and linked combinations describe those restrictions.

    CASA parameter CASA default casa-rs default; mapping Implementation Scope and runtime restrictions Evidence
    vis '' ''; vis → vis Partial Contract 38: a list of MeasurementSets images as one, as CASA's tclean images its vis list. Each is opened and surveyed with its own selectors (one per MeasurementSet, or one or a list of one for all; another length is refused with CASA's message), ordered stably by its earliest MAIN TIME and visited as CASA visits its ms0, ms1, ... keys, as text (input_parameters.checkAndFixSelectionPars, mslisthelper.sort_mslist, imager_base.selectData); one selecting no sample is left out unless it is the first (SynthesisImagerVi2::selectData). The image's coordinates (obs date, telescope, phase centre, rest frequency, a cube's axis) come from CASA's msobj (buildCoordinateSystem): the first MeasurementSet, or for a cube or cubedata whose start is a frequency or velocity the last one whose selected band holds it (CAS-12877); a continuum band, and so the MFS and MT-MFS reference frequency, spans every one. A cube reads each other MeasurementSet's window where it reaches the cube, and one that does not reach it is read so its MODEL_DATA holds the zero prediction, as CASA writes it. Weighting density spans every MeasurementSet; datacolumn falls back to DATA per MeasurementSet; savemodel writes each one's MODEL_DATA (or virtual model). CASA's addwtspec, which adds WEIGHT_SPECTRUM to a listed MeasurementSet lacking it as a Vi/VB2 workaround, is not done: casa-rs reads each MeasurementSet's own weights, which that column copies, and never modifies its inputs. A row's spectral window and field are keyed among every MeasurementSet's, as if they were concatenated, so the mosaic keeps each one's beams (CASA's msId_spw, SimplePBConvFunc::convIndex) and a mosweight density each one's fields (VisImagingWeight's msId_field) apart; its dishes are every MeasurementSet's aperture classes (HetArrayConvFunc::findAntennaSizes). Refused with several MeasurementSets (#980): the awproject gridder (its catalog keys cells on the first MeasurementSet's frequencies), a moving phase centre, specmode cubesource, fitspw and a cube's per-channel density weighting. A cube with an unset start reads the band selected over every MeasurementSet, on the first one's channels (SynthesisParamsImage::buildCoordinateSystem). selection

FIELD CODE/numeric-name fallback, quoted/regex/comparison/extended-glob expressions and full numeric-ID validation remain unfinished. Contract 38: one selector per MeasurementSet of the vis list, or one (or a list of one) for every MeasurementSet; a list of another length is refused with CASA's message. A command-line text is one selector; a list is written as one or by repeating the flag. selection

    usemask 'user' 'user'; usemask → usemask Implemented user, pb and auto-multithresh pass; controls whether user mask is read. usemask='pb' keeps, in every channel and polarization, the pixels whose .pb exceeds pbmask (SDMaskHandler::makePBMask), formed once at the first boundary from the weight image's beam under a response and the telescope's analytic beam otherwise; mask is not read. auto-multithresh follows SDMaskHandler::autoMaskByMultiThreshold plane by plane, every channel and Stokes plane of its own: the threshold mask pruned, smoothed and cut, the inherited mask grown through the low-noise threshold, and the negative mask; a cube is masked in tclean's serial channel blocks, each smoothed by the common beam of its planes' PSF beams after blank PSFs inherit the largest fitted beam in the full image domain, even across blocks; every plane thresholded by the image's PSF sidelobe level, and a channel's planes stop together (skipChannels); a plane with no data keeps its mask. Its statistics, peak, pruned regions and support read only the pixels the residual's pixel mask keeps, the .pb's above pblimit where pblimit is not negative (SDMaskHandler::autoMask, #1079), for every gridder: the weight image's beam under a response, the telescope's analytic beam otherwise. At the first boundary of a run with nsigma its median and noise are calcRobustRMS's, inside the .pb above pbmask. Under a pbmask above zero its pixel mask is pb > pbmask, whatever pblimit's sign, which also bounds the mask it makes (SDMaskHandler::autoMaskWithinPB). deconvolution
    Catalog domain: auto-multithresh, pb, user    		 signature, reader, pb-statistics-20261103, pb-masks-20261103
    mask '' ''; mask → mask_box or mask_image Partial One mask image, or CRTF pixel boxes and circles, inline or in a region file, each optionally limited to a spectral range=[...] in frequency or pixels (CASA's channel rounding; a region whose range misses the image is left out). A range over part of a cube masks its channels only, a plane per channel. Bounds clip to the image and outside/empty-region unions mean zero support, not full-plane support. 38 small paired rows pass across CPU64/CPU32/Metal32 and public Python; explicit empty-file + fresh numbering preserves old products and adds no components. Absent masks still permit normal cleaning. A mask image is expanded to the image's planes as SDMaskHandler::expandMask does (a continuum Stokes I mask to every plane, a continuum mask of the image's Stokes planes to every channel, a cube Stokes I mask to every Stokes plane) and refused where CASA refuses; regridding a mask along frequency or Stokes (copyMask) is refused (issue 980). Fractional/world/general CRTF, multiple images, new masks on occupied default-restart outputs and broader mask/mode combinations remain unfinished. deconvolution

    Catalog domain: bool    		 signature, reader, continuation-20261102, psfcutoff-restart-20261105
    psfcutoff 0.35 0.35; psfcutoff → psfcutoff Implemented The requested cutoff fits a newly formed PSF and sets the beam used for minor-cycle sidelobes, Clark patches, automatic masking and publication. A complete existing PSF beam set is reused when calcpsf=False, and on MFS recomputation; cube gridding refits after discarding temporary PSF metadata. A headerless saved PSF is fitted at the default 0.35 before cleaning. Off-centre saved beams are subtracted at the image centre; an off-centre plane with no saved beam still uses its peak as its sidelobe pending #1140 item 6.     tclean interacting modes and task behavior¶

    Selection and multi-MS inputs¶

    Partial — vis, field, spw, timerange, uvrange, antenna, scan, observation, intent, datacolumn and selectdata (#54).

    IDs/ranges, names/simple patterns, mixed field lists, SPW/channel text, intent and UV limits are routed natively. Sixteen field cases compare exact physical MAIN row order or refusal with CASA, including flagged and padded FIELD metadata. Since the main imaging cutover was integrated (3016f1b), FIELD selection resolves over the observation catalogue's FIELD rows on MSv2 and MSv4 alike; on the production traversal the field cases of the CASA row oracle (IDs, names, glob, mixed, range) and both refusals read exactly CASA's physical MAIN rows. Production T1 compares ID/name selections with selectdata=False on the single native route. Scan and observation selection is active from tclean contract 33 (owner decision 2026-11-02): on that path scan 1, scan 3 and observation 0 read exactly CASA's rows, and T1 images each numeric selection as its equivalent FIELD selection with either selectdata value and refuses CASA's refused forms without writing images. Through the public Python task, 13 science cases and 23 CASA grammar cases pass against CASA on CPU64, CPU32 and Metal32 (108/108). CODE/numeric-name fallback, advanced field forms, richer scan/observation grammar, remain unfinished; a vis list images as one observation from contract 38, as CASA's tclean does (awproject with several MeasurementSets is refused, #980); antenna and timerange are active from contract 34 (timerange on MeasurementSets only). Image-science comparisons for selected real datasets, flags, channel membership and data-column fallback remain separate required evidence.

    Evidence: reader, representative, field-selection, cloud-continuation, scan-observation-activation, vis-list-20261102.

    Cube axes, reference frequencies and moving sources¶

    Partial — specmode, spw, start, width, nchan, reffreq, restfreq, outframe, veltype, interpolation and perchanweightdensity.

    nchan=-1 takes CASA's count from the start or the first selected channel to the band's far edge in channel, frequency and radio-velocity grids, and interpolation='cubic' fits casacore's four-point cubic (#1066); an optical-velocity grid, which CASA tabulates, is refused (#1096); a cube over several selected windows lays its axis over their channels merged as CASA's MSTransformRegridder::combineSpwsCore merges them, grids every window's rows onto it, and with nchan=-1 counts a channel grid's planes over the merged band as regridChanBounds does (#980); MFS outframe (LSRK/BARY/TOPO, header only, gridding stays LSRK as in CASA) and restfreq lists (first active, the others listed in the header) are read; MFS REST outframe and blank restfreq entries remain restricted. Moving-source cubesource needs its own representative oracle (#714). Fresh standard MVC uses cube major cycles and Taylor minor cycles. The standard multi-SPW dirty and clean comparisons pass, with remaining cases on #1069. The moving-MFS spectral envelope now uses CASA FIELD-origin direction semantics; six fixed/moving CPU64/CPU32/GPU32 comparisons pass recorded WCS values, and the previous 3.5 Hz increment mismatch is repaired at its unchanged tolerance. This does not complete moving-PB or cubesource acceptance. The ordinary MFS default SOURCE rest-frequency label is now repaired; five fresh mosaic/AW weight families and small restart metadata checks match. DOPPLER-backed SOURCE lookup and multiple SOURCE key groups with stored wildcards are explicitly unfinished, not approved deviations. The MFS header's first visibility field is the least (ARRAY_ID, DATA_DESC_ID, FIELD_ID) of the selected samples, CASA's buffer order: the MSv2 adapter states each partition's array and the survey reads the rows' arrays for a partition holding several (3bdf6fa, after Astra's review found the merge had dropped ARRAY_ID); a T1 run with opposing arrays records CASA's choice. An MSv4 has no arrays. The moving-MFS increment oracle on the catalogue path gives CASA's 125020996.11132812 Hz exactly (8b0fe44).

    Evidence: reader, representative, mvc-decision, taylor-pb-mfs, approved-comparisons-restart, cloud-continuation, cube-axes-20261103.

    Gridder, weighting and beam combinations¶

    Partial — standard/W/mosaic/AW, rectangular geometry, facets, normtype, beam terms and density weighting.

    Rectangular standard/W/mosaic and uvtaper were implemented in #970. AW remains square/equal-cell, and images MFS, MT-MFS and cubes whose selected windows hold one channel each; multi-channel cubes also run with nearest interpolation in the data or LSRK frame (each plane its own weight image, issue 719; tracked TOPO/BARY, rest, linear and cubic cases wait for issue 1121); it reads tclean's aterm, psterm, wbawp, conjbeams and any positive wprojplanes (#980), and an imported CASA cache must already exist: where cfcache names a missing directory CASA fills it, casa-rs refuses (aw_cf_source=native-evla generates its own). The imported two-field test_mosaic_mtmfs caches agree with CASA for both beam choices, including pointing with uniformly odd cache buffers (#1214, #1070). Mixed-parity caches use each selected buffer's own pointing origin, avoiding CASA's reuse of a wider gradient array (#1070). On same-prefix continuation CASA can multiply the saved higher Taylor weight planes again. casa-rs recomputes the weights with the PSF and agrees with fresh CASA controls (#1315, #1070). Continuation with calcpsf=False,calcres=True reuses the saved AW PSF and weights (#1317); two-term PB correction is also supported in this case (#1318). Mosaic per-field density weighting (mosweight) is implemented, continuum and a cube's per-channel density (BriggsCubeWeightor multiField, #818); W/AW facets and additional gridders/weightings remain incomplete. Native CF generation is verified separately from CASA cache oracles. The native write_pb default is True. Homogeneous ALMA-labelled 7m arrays select ACA; legacy VLA PB now covers all named bands and NVSS frequency gaps. Fresh ordinary-default W/Hogbom20 CPU64/CPU32 runs pass all seven products, with PB NRMS zero. EVLA/VLA mosaics grid with CASA's common EVLA primary beam (HetArrayConvFunc COMMONPB, PBMath1DEVLA) at the first buffer's LSRK frequencies and MosaicFT's sector edges; conjbeams on one-plane mosaics grids with CASA's widened, resampled conjugate cell (#980). Moving/nonconstant FIELD directions, mixed dish classes, unsupported telescopes and remaining EVLA bands are unfinished. Explicit GPU W/AW remains refused. CASA Float channel-frequency narrowing is now matched in shared Taylor factors. All five fresh mosaic/native-AW MT-MFS rows pass the owner-approved scoped weak weight.tt1 rule and retained original recorded checks; 3 override only raw weight.tt1 relative-error failures. Exact guards and 1e-6 matrix-scale limits remain. Full metadata is separate. Existing #690 odd-sumwt scaling remains distinct. Legacy VLA cube PB takes CASA's channel-zero aperture scaled per channel: both T1.5 VLA cube rows give .pb NRMS 0.0 at 9768bb1, 3016f1b and 2628d8b (pre-candidate 0.0136); independent review pending. Cube Briggs density cells now use CASA's negated u and v (2628d8b): all 3,056,967 point-cube-briggs (row, channel) weights match CASA's to 1.35e-7, where 7 exact Float ties previously read a neighbouring cell.

    Evidence: reader, additions, representative, default-products, compensated-current, taylor-pb-mfs, approved-comparisons-restart, cloud-continuation.

Independent default-continuation and migration tests pass. ASP, MEM, MT-MFS nterms=1, PB masks/pbsquare, several auto-mask controls, explicit restoring beams are incomplete or refused. Compare convergence/flux/beam and exact mask support separately; nonlinear component identity is not required, but approved mode-specific gates stay binding. The owner accepted only the original identity-bound b6a87 clean8/cpu32 weak model result; raw model.tt1 NRMS 0.0012759712 remains recorded. No general weak-model waiver or automatic future-output acceptance exists. Explicit all-zero image masks now stop with CASA code 7 for the verified standard-MFS cases; existing nonzero models are preserved on empty-mask restart. Broader mode combinations still require evidence. General regions and other mask combinations remain unfinished. A plane whose published .sumwt is positive but at most CASA's 1e-07 bound is now empty as in CASA (zero PSF, residual, model and image; nothing cleaned), verified by a paired constructed-weight cube probe (8fcee92).

    tclean deviations and unfinished work¶

    interactive-headless: Agreed for now¶
    • Inputs: interactive=True; graphical mask editing and pause/resume.
    • CASA: Uses casaviewer where available; 6.7.6.14 on macOS raises when the viewer is absent.
    • casa-rs: Refuses interactive=True explicitly; accepts False. Supply a mask or use supported auto-multithresh controls.
    • Rationale: The application is headless; the owner accepted the exception for the current phase.
    • User impact: No graphical mask editing/pause-resume. Future interactive operation remains wanted, not permanently out of scope.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Headless exception for now, but ultimately we will want to do something for this. Recorded on #980.
    • Evidence: interactive-decision, reader.
    mvc: Unfinished¶
    • Inputs: specmode='mvc'.
    • CASA: Supports the MVC task path.
    • casa-rs: Supports the bounded fresh standard-gridder mode. The three-SPW dirty, clean and PB-corrected clean references agree on all published images, masks, coordinates and controller counts.
    • Rationale: The Taylor header spans the first and last published cube channel centres and preserves the cube rest-frequency label. The polynomial expansion has its own reference frequency.
    • User impact: Continuation, start models and other gridders remain on #1069. The separate dirty alpha case agrees with CASA after correcting its standard-grid sector bounds.
    • Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
    • Evidence: mvc-decision, reader.
    restart: Unfinished¶
    • Inputs: Any occupied main/outlier image namespace; restart=False or recalculation controls.
    • CASA: Continues existing products by default, or chooses a numbered fresh prefix with restart=False.
    • casa-rs: Bounded same-grid standard Stokes-I MFS default continuation now has native gates, small paired CASA 10->0->10/mask evidence and both-direction continuation. Contract32 activates single-main fresh numbering; small public products/preservation pass. Other occupied namespaces, coordinated outlier/orphan numbering and calc flags remain unfinished.
    • Rationale: Reuse the existing native model/mask lifecycle with admitted ownership, preserving unsupported-state refusals while completing parity.
    • User impact: The bounded native default path can continue prior work; unsupported modes and flags still require completion and are not agreed exclusions.
    • Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
    • Evidence: lifecycle, reader, restart-native-current, approved-comparisons-restart, numbering-activation.
    uvtaper-units: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: A unitless uvtaper entry.
    • CASA: Interprets bare values as radians, despite documenting lambda; large values may zero every imaging weight.
    • casa-rs: Requires explicit angle or lambda/klambda units and errors clearly on bare values.
    • Rationale: Prevent a documented-unit mismatch from silently producing an empty image.
    • User impact: Callers must supply units; numerical unit-bearing taper behavior remains governed by #970 evidence.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Require explicit units.
    • Evidence: additions, reader.
    startmodel-stokes: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: Start-model Stokes labels differ from the target; equal plane counts.
    • CASA: Copies planes by position without converting polarization, since only direction and spectral axes are regridded.
    • casa-rs: Copies equal-count planes by position and warns; focused native warning/permuted-plane tests pass, and the single Q-to-I case is paired with CASA 6.7.6.14. Multi-plane paired and unequal-count behavior remain unverified.
    • Rationale: Match CASA's behavior, while making the label mismatch visible.
    • User impact: A Q model can seed I and permuted Stokes planes are copied positionally; this is not a physical polarization conversion. Unequal-plane-count behavior still needs oracle assessment.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Match but issue a warning. This supersedes the prior refusal.
    • Evidence: additions, reader, default-products.
    restart-absolute-directory: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: restart=False with an absolute imagename and existing products.
    • CASA: 6.7.6.14 incrementImageNameList slices dirname[2:], corrupting an absolute directory before creating the next numbered image; the probe fails to create its PSF.
    • casa-rs: Activated native contract32 keeps the correct absolute directory; alternating absolute/relative public calls choose and publish the expected fresh prefixes.
    • Rationale: Avoid a reproducible CASA path-handling bug while preserving the intended numbering behavior.
    • User impact: Numbered products stay in the requested directory. Broader restart acceptance remains open.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Use the correct absolute directory. Recorded on #944.
    • Evidence: lifecycle, numbering-preview, numbering-activation.
    native-write-pb-default: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: Ordinary casa-rs tclean calls that omit the casa-rs-only write_pb option; profiles from contract 29 or earlier.
    • CASA: Ordinary supported tclean imaging creates a .pb product; CASA has no write_pb parameter.
    • casa-rs: Contract 30 defaults the extra write_pb option to True. Explicit False remains an opt-out; migrating an omitted old default warns and adopts True. All 92 CASA parameters/defaults and pbcor=False are unchanged.
    • Rationale: Make ordinary product production agree with CASA without patching CASA or changing its parameter defaults.
    • User impact: The PB file is now produced within implemented PB scope. Moving-field, telescope and frequency limitations remain unfinished work and can currently require explicit False.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Approve the casa-rs default change.
    • Evidence: default-products, reader.
    hogbom-default: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: deconvolver='hogbom' with a positive minor-cycle iteration limit.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6.14 may commit one extra component while clamping its reported count to the requested limit.
    • casa-rs: Contract31 defaults the native extra policy to casa-inclusive; explicit strict remains available. Omitted older profile defaults warn and adopt the new value, while explicit values are preserved.
    • Rationale: Match CASA's reported count and scientific products using the existing implementation.
    • User impact: Positive Hogbom cycles may apply one extra component while reporting the CASA count. Other solvers retain their canonical strict inactive policy. No CASA parameter/default changes.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Approved both proposed adoptions, including CASA-compatible Hogbom counting by default.
    • Evidence: compensated-current, adopted-defaults.
    weak-taylor-weight-comparison: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: Two-term Stokes-I MFS mosaic/AW weight.tt1; both reference weak-coupling measures<=1e-3, with all reviewed accumulator guards.
    • CASA: An almost cancelling odd weight term makes error relative to its own near-zero norm unstable; exact CASA product values remain the oracle.
    • casa-rs: The approved gate uses E2 across valid pixels and Emax on reference-only PB support, each<=1e-6 of the Taylor matrix scale. All other original checks remain. Three raw NRMS failures are visible scoped overrides; outputs are unchanged.
    • Rationale: Judge a weak coupling against its surrounding positive Taylor matrix while preserving PSD, masks, finiteness and other scientific checks.
    • User impact: An odd term below the matrix-scale error budget could be lost and still pass this one criterion. No model, metadata or arbitrary-mode waiver.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-11-01, this chat: Approve the scoped comparison rule.
    • Evidence: approved-comparisons-restart.
    specific-weak-mtmfs-model-output: Previously agreed¶

    • CASA: The weak model.tt1 coefficient is -0.00026645659818314016 Jy; the original raw comparison remains available.

    • Rationale: A mathematically checked component solve and exact support show that this specific coefficient discrepancy has a much smaller represented in-band intensity effect.
    • User impact: The raw failure remains; this exact reviewed output is accepted. Future outputs have no automatic approval, and runtime visibility prediction remains separately unverified.
    • Owner decision: Owner, 2026-11-01, this chat: Accept this specific reviewed output.
    • Evidence: standard-mtmfs-current, approved-comparisons-restart.

Every deconvolver, cubes, Stokes, outliers, masks, thresholds, n-sigma, restarts, start models and dirty runs, with CASA's own test_iterbot fullsummary calls. Differences: the recorded full-Stokes cube channel-0 near-tie (two cases), issue 1126 (clark on several Stokes planes), issue 1127 (a seeded mosaic cube, two cases) and one stop code on a plane with no data under nsigma. The products weren't compared here.

    Tools¶

    Each CASA class casa-rs presents in Python, against the signatures recorded from CASA 6.7.6.14 (crates/casars-python/python/tests/data/casa_<class>_signatures.json), which the Python tests hold the classes to. A reviewed class has a file under resources/casa-compat/semantics/tools/ giving each method's status, scope and evidence; the others are not reviewed yet. The simulation classes (simulator, componentlist, measures, quanta and simutil) are reviewed in #1053, and image, coordsys, table, msmetadata and ms in #1025.

    Class Python CASA methods Reviewed Implemented Partial Not implemented Disabled in CASA Deprecated in CASA Unassessed
    componentlist casars.tools 61 yes 54 3 4 0 0 0
    coordsys casars.tools 69 no
    image casars.tools 103 no
    measures casars.tools 48 yes 24 7 17 0 0 0
    ms casars.tools 77 no
    msmetadata casars.tools 103 no
    quanta casars.tools 57 yes 55 0 2 0 0 0
    simulator casars.tools 33 yes 20 9 0 3 1 0
    simutil casars.simutil 57 yes 46 2 9 0 0 0
    table casars.tools 83 no

    componentlist¶

    componentlist method implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise NotImplementedError naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

    Audit baseline: 49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947. Status counts: Implemented: 54, Partial: 3, Not implemented: 4, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

    componentlist interacting modes and tool behavior¶

    componentlist deviations and unfinished work¶

    limb-darkened-disk-no-exponent: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Writing a limb-darkened disk without an exponent aborts CASA
    • Inputs: cl.fromrecord for a limb-darkened disk without optionalParameters, then cl.close.
    • CASA: writeTable stores an empty optional-parameter vector and throws casacore::indexError from the ComponentList destructor, terminating the process (CASA 6.7.6: close aborts); readTable would read out of bounds.
    • casa-rs: Leaves the cell undefined, read as the shape's default exponent 0.05.
    • Rationale: The default exponent is what every undescribed row already reads as.
    • User impact: Such lists save and reload instead of aborting the process.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    single-spectral-index-read-past-end: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: A single spectral index is read past its end
    • Inputs: cl.addcomponent(spectrumtype='spectral index', index=alpha) on a polarised component, then predict.
    • CASA: The matrix sampleStokes used by every component prediction has no one-index branch and reads indices 1 to 3 out of bounds; the per-frequency version scales only I (read from source, not probed).
    • casa-rs: Reads a single index as [alpha,0,0,0], so fractional polarisation is constant.
    • Rationale: This is what the matrix sampler's comments describe.
    • User impact: Polarised components with one index predict Q, U, V reliably instead of scaled by whatever follows the vector.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    addcomponent-four-flux-as-stokes: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: cl.addcomponent stores four flux values as Stokes
    • Inputs: cl.addcomponent(flux=[1,0.1,0.2,0.9], polarization='circular').
    • CASA: setflux stores four values with FluxRep::setValue(Vector), which resets the representation to Stokes, so the values become I,Q,U,V rather than RR,RL,LR,LL (probed with CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Does the same (owner decision 2026-10-23), including raising for an unknown name or a non-Stokes representation with one value.
    • Rationale: Scripts get the list CASA would give them.
    • User impact: A circular or linear four-value flux is silently read as Stokes in both implementations.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-23 (#841), recorded in docs/CASA (C++) bugs.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    addcomponent-flux-single-precision: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: cl.addcomponent stores the flux in single precision
    • Inputs: cl.addcomponent(flux=0.1).
    • CASA: setflux reads values into a Vector (single precision), so flux=0.1 is stored as 0.10000000149011612 (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Keeps the values given.
    • Rationale: Avoids a needless single-precision rounding.
    • User impact: Fluxes differ from CASA's by at most 6e-8 relative.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    addcomponent-point-for-bad-shape: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: cl.addcomponent keeps a point for a shape it cannot make
    • Inputs: cl.addcomponent with a misspelled shape name, a limb-darkened disk, or an axis that is not an angle.
    • CASA: addcomponent ignores setshape's False return, so the component stays a point and addcomponent returns True (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Does the same and logs at ERROR; what setshape raises, addcomponent raises too after removing the component.
    • Rationale: Scripts that misspell a shape get points, as in CASA.
    • User impact: A misspelled shape yields a point source instead of an error.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    isphysical-empty-list-exit: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: componentlist().isphysical(), default [-1] selection on an empty list.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6-14 exits the Python process without raising an exception.
    • casa-rs: Raises a catchable RuntimeError; an explicit empty index list still returns True.
    • Rationale: A component check should not terminate its caller.
    • User impact: Callers can recover rather than lose the Python session.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved a catchable error on 2026-11-05 (#1158).
    • Evidence: isphysical-casa.
    iscomponentlist-cyclic-input: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: A self-referential Python list passed to componentlist.iscomponentlist.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6-14 terminates the Python process with SIGSEGV.
    • casa-rs: Logs the placeholder warning and returns False without recursing forever.
    • Rationale: A type-check placeholder should not terminate its caller.
    • User impact: Callers retain the Python session.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved correcting CASA crashes as a class on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: iscomponentlist-casa.

    measures¶

    measures method implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise NotImplementedError naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

    Audit baseline: 49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947. Status counts: Implemented: 24, Partial: 7, Not implemented: 17, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

    CASA method CASA parameters Implementation Scope Evidence
    addxvalue a Partial (#1053) Returns direction cosines or the position vector for scalar supported records, and an empty record for epoch and frequency. Measure offsets, multivalued records and unsupported measure kinds are refused explicitly; cyclic or excessively deep records are rejected safely. signature, addxvalue-casa
    asbaseline pos Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053. CASA's simulation tasks and simutil do not call it. signature, refusals
    baseline rf='ITRF', v0=[], v1=[], v2=[], off={} Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053. CASA's simulation tasks and simutil do not call it. signature, refusals
    cometangdiam Partial (#1053) Returns CASA's -1 rad sentinel when no comet table is loaded. Loading comet ephemeris tables via framecomet remains unimplemented. signature, no-comet-casa
    cometdist Partial (#1053) Returns CASA's -1 AU sentinel when no comet table is loaded. Loading comet ephemeris tables via framecomet remains unimplemented. signature, no-comet-casa
    cometname Partial (#1053) Returns the empty name and CASA warning when no comet table is loaded. Loading comet ephemeris tables via framecomet remains unimplemented. signature, no-comet-casa

    measures interacting modes and tool behavior¶

    measures deviations and unfinished work¶

    measures-tool-default-frame: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: The measures tool converts with a frame at MJD 0 and the geocentre
    • Inputs: me.measure(me.direction('J2000','12h00m00','45d00m00'),'AZEL') in a fresh measures tool, with no doframe call.
    • CASA: The tool starts with a default frame (MJD 0 UTC, zero-vector position, J2000 pole) and converts using it without a warning, returning AZEL for 1858-11-17 at the Earth's centre.
    • casa-rs: Starts with an empty frame, so the conversion fails with 'Cannot convert due to missing frame information' until doframe sets the element.
    • Rationale: A conversion should not silently use placeholder epoch and position.
    • User impact: A script that forgot doframe gets an error rather than a meaningless answer.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    iau2000-keeps-iau1980-sidereal: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: IAU 2000 keeps the IAU 1980 sidereal time and ecliptic obliquities
    • Inputs: Conversions to HADEC, AZEL, ITRF, MECLIPTIC or TECLIPTIC with measures.iau2000.b_use set.
    • CASA: casacore still forms GAST from IAU 1982 GMST and the IAU 1980 equation of the equinoxes, and uses IAU 1980 obliquities (fundArg T^2 term -0.0059 arcsec, 0.37 mas in MECLIPTIC by 2026).
    • casa-rs: Computes all of these as casacore does (casacore_sidereal, casacore_frames).
    • Rationale: Follows casacore for parity; it moved IAU 2000A J2000 to HADEC from 20.4 to 16.7 mas off casacore.
    • User impact: Results under IAU 2000 match casacore, including its remaining IAU 1980 pieces.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    direction-latitude-unnormalized: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Direction latitudes come from an unnormalized vector
    • Inputs: Direction conversions that use the galactic or FK4 matrices, e.g. B1950 to GALACTIC or J2000 to B1950 near a pole.
    • CASA: The 10-digit literal matrices are orthogonal only to about 7e-11 and doConvert does not renormalize, so getLat's asin(z) is off by (|v|-1)tan(b) and becomes NaN once z exceeds 1.
    • casa-rs: Converts with the same matrices, renormalizes and reports the latitude of the unit vector.
    • Rationale: asin(z) assumes a unit vector.
    • User impact: Reported angles differ by up to 51 microarcsec (1.6 mas at b=-89.77 deg) while directions agree to 0.01 microarcsec.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    iau2000-jmean-j2000-order: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: IAU 2000 JMEAN to J2000 applies bias and precession in the wrong order
    • Inputs: MECLIPTIC or TECLIPTIC to J2000 conversion under IAU 2000.
    • CASA: deapplyPrecession applies B then P giving PBv', not the inverse BPv', so JMEAN to J2000 is not the inverse of J2000 to JMEAN (0.14 mas off in 2026, growing).
    • casa-rs: Inverts exactly.
    • Rationale: The reverse transform must invert the forward one.
    • User impact: Round trips through the IAU 2000 ecliptics differ from casacore by 0.14 mas in 2026.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    iau2000-gmst1-tt-fraction: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: IAU 2000 GMST1 adds the TT day fraction
    • Inputs: UT1 to GMST1 (and so LMST) under IAU 2000.
    • CASA: UT1_GMST1 adds GMST00/(2pi), already a sidereal time of day, to the TT epoch, counting the TT day fraction twice: UT1 51544.5 gives GMST1 58258.280 against 58257.779, and GMST1_UT1 does not undo it.
    • casa-rs: Uses the IAU 1976 GMST1 under either model, as casacore's own GAST does.
    • Rationale: This keeps GMST1, LMST, GAST and LAST consistent with each other.
    • User impact: LMST differs from casacore's IAU 2000 value but agrees with casacore under IAU 1976.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.

    quanta¶

    quanta method implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise NotImplementedError naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

    Audit baseline: 49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947. Status counts: Implemented: 55, Partial: 0, Not implemented: 2, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

    quanta interacting modes and tool behavior¶

    quanta deviations and unfinished work¶

    quantities-non-conforming-units: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: The tools convert quantities whose units do not conform
    • Inputs: integrationtime='10Jy', integrationtime='10m' or freq='1s' passed to a simulator method; a direction string such as 'J2000 12h00m00 garbage'.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6 converts without checking conformance: '10Jy' becomes 1e-25 s, '10m' 10 s, '1s' 1 Hz, and an unreadable direction token keeps the previous value (declination 180 deg).
    • casa-rs: Refuses both with casacore's 'From/to units not consistent' or a parse error, while keeping CASA's cross-dimension conversions (number is SI, angle is time, length is frequency via c).
    • Rationale: Non-conforming units are almost certainly user errors and should not become silent wrong values.
    • User impact: Scripts with a wrong unit now stop with an error instead of running with a nonsense value.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.

    quanta evidence¶

    Evidence is scoped to its named revision, inputs and comparator. Historical reported results need applicability review; planned or unexecuted tests are not passes.

    signature¶

    simulator¶

    simulator method implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise NotImplementedError naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

    Audit baseline: 30517424209d7531ce6f4c93e1e8ef94a5e4e66b. Status counts: Implemented: 20, Partial: 9, Not implemented: 0, Disabled in CASA: 3, Deprecated in CASA: 1, Unassessed: 0.

    CASA method CASA parameters Implementation Scope Evidence
    close Implemented Writes and closes the MeasurementSet, within the session's memory budget. CASA aborts here after a mosaic predict; casa-rs does not (decision). signature, binding-tests, observe-20261103, predict-multiple-models-20261111
    corrupt Partial (#1053) Applies the selected noise, gain, leakage, tropospheric and applied-table terms. Each corruption step skips a spectral window any Jones term has no solution for, including its noise and corrected-data updates; predictions and later covered steps persist. Later scans reuse the original noise solutions and original window coverage; supplied-table families outside setapply scope remain unfinished. signature, binding-tests, noise-20261103, corruptions-20261103, troposphere-20261103, refusals, flagged-samples-20261112, selection-ordered-data-20261112, corruption-window-coverage-20261112, noise-troposphere-completion
    done Implemented As close, and frees the tool. signature, binding-tests, predict-multiple-models-20261111

    setapply table='', type='', t=0.0, field=[], interp='linear', calwt=False, spwmap=[-1], opacity=0.0 Partial (#1291) Applies G/D/B/Df, scalar T/Tf, full polarized J/Jf, general Dgen/Dfgen leakage and X/Xf/GlinXph/GlinXphf polarized phase gains, and supplied K/Kcross/KMBD/KAntPos/TOpac/TfOpac/EGainCurve/EPowerCurve/B TSYS tables.     simulator interacting modes and tool behavior¶

    simulator deviations and unfinished work¶

    airy-blockage-ratio: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: The Airy beam's blockage term uses the inverse length ratio
    • Inputs: Any Airy primary beam with a central blockage, e.g. ALMA's default 10.7 m dish with 0.75 m blockage via setvp.
    • CASA: PBMath1DAiry::fillPBArray evaluates the blockage term at xD/b instead of xb/D, so main-lobe power is about 1% above the physical pattern (0.507 against 0.500 at the half-power radius).
    • casa-rs: Tabulates the same expression (AnnularApertureVoltageTable), matching CASA's predictions to 1.6e-7.
    • Rationale: ALMA's 10.7 m effective diameter was chosen against CASA's beam.
    • User impact: Beam-dependent predictions and images agree with CASA but are about 1% off the physical pattern.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    tai-stored-as-utc: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: The simulator stores TAI as UTC
    • Inputs: sm.observe with a reference epoch; the stored TIME column.
    • CASA: observe adds TAI seconds to the offsets and writes the result into TIME labelled UTC, so data start TAI-UTC (37 s since 2017) after the requested epoch; geometry reads the number as UT1.
    • casa-rs: Stores the requested UTC and also reads the stored TIME as UT1 for geometry, so uvw agree with CASA at equal TIME.
    • Rationale: The stored value should match its MEASINFO label; parity runs offset the start by TAI-UTC taken from the measures data.
    • User impact: Schedules and simulated calibration tables differ from CASA's by TAI-UTC (37 s today) unless the start is shifted.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision 5 of the simulation plan, accepted 2026-10-20 (docs/simulation/simulation-plan-20261020.md): TIME honours the requested UTC.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    shadowing-antenna-in-front: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: The simulator charges shadowing to the antenna in front
    • Inputs: sm.observe over antennas close enough to shadow each other.
    • CASA: NewMSSimulator::blockage leaves the blockage on the antenna in front (the one doing the blocking), whereas CASA's own flagger FlagAgentShadow flags the antenna behind.
    • casa-rs: Flags the antenna behind (ShadowedAntenna::Behind); the CASA convention remains available as CasaSimulatorFront.
    • Rationale: CASA's own flagger shows the intent: the antenna behind is the shadowed one.
    • User impact: Shadowed rows are flagged on the opposite antenna from CASA's simulator unless CasaSimulatorFront is selected.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    setspwindow-relabels-frame: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: setspwindow relabels every spectral window's frame
    • Inputs: setspwindow called twice with different frames, e.g. a TOPO window then an LSRK one.
    • CASA: Each call ends with measFreqRef().fillColumn(freqType), writing the new frame into MEAS_FREQ_REF for every row, so both windows end up labelled LSRK.
    • casa-rs: Writes each window's own frame.
    • Rationale: Frequencies were given in different frames, so each window must keep its own label.
    • User impact: Earlier windows get a wrong frame label in SPECTRAL_WINDOW.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    setconfig-unconvertible-coordsystem: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: setconfig accepts coordinate systems it cannot convert
    • Inputs: setconfig(coordsystem='longlat') or an unknown coordinate system name.
    • CASA: longlat2global logs SEVERE 'not yet implemented' (or initAnt 'Unknown coordinate system type'), the antennas are added at the Earth's centre, and setconfig returns True.
    • casa-rs: Raises RuntimeError for longlat; an unknown name logs CASA's message and returns False; neither adds antennas.
    • Rationale: An array with meaningless geometry should not be silently built.
    • User impact: A script continues with a meaningless array in CASA but stops or sees False in casa-rs.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.

    negative-component-under-beam: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: A negative component turns positive under the primary beam
    • Inputs: sm.setvp(dovp=True) with a negative-flux component, e.g. -0.4 Jy point 20 arcsec off centre.
    • CASA: The fullspectral beam path multiplies by a spectrum sample over abs(flux), restoring the sign twice, so a negative flux comes out positive (+0.4 Jy times the beam).
    • casa-rs: Keeps the sign.

    tsys-manual-stale-or-borrowed: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: tsys-manual noise takes stale or borrowed system temperatures
    • Inputs: setnoise(mode='tsys-manual') then corrupt, especially at low elevation, with rows observed after setnoise, changed integration time or shadowed antennas.
    • CASA: The airmass cache is up to 100 s stale (variance sawtooth 1.01 to 1.56), later scans take the nearest earlier integration's solution, every row takes the first row's EXPOSURE, and an antenna without an unflagged row applies as 1.

    tsys-manual-scales-every-noise-term: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: A tsys-manual term scales every noise term
    • Inputs: Two tsys-manual terms, or simplenoise plus tsys-manual, then corrupt.
    • CASA: collapseForSim adds every ANoise term then applies every NoiseScale T term to DATA, so each term's scale multiplies all noise: variance 4 sigma^4 for two terms, and a 0.5 Jy simplenoise comes out scaled by the system temperature.
    • casa-rs: Adds each term's noise with its own sigma, so simplenoise stays in Jy and terms of one seed still add coherently.
    • Rationale: Owner: follow CASA only if its product is the better simulation, which it is not; powers of uncorrelated noise add.

    settrop-dispersive-wet-delay: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: settrop turns the phase by the dispersive wet delay alone
    • Inputs: sm.settrop with default pwv and deltapwv.
    • CASA: cphase takes the zenith wet phase delay from getDispersiveH2OPhaseDelay (the dispersive part alone), which is 940, 119, 36, 23 and 8.5 times smaller than the whole wet delay at 43, 100, 183, 230 and 345 GHz.

    settrop-screen-anisotropic: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: settrop's screen has an anisotropic spectrum
    • Inputs: settrop(mode='screen') on a screen long along the wind (e.g. 1359 by 271 pixels).
    • CASA: fBM::initialize uses index rather than wavenumber (stretching the spectrum along the wind), does not wrap the full axis, uses exponent -(beta+1/2)/2 (comment: 'still something not quite right'), and sets values past the half axis's end, copying the last two pixels from past the output.
    • casa-rs: Draws |k|^-(beta+1)/2 on wrapped wavenumbers in cycles per pixel with proper conjugate pairs and transforms every pixel, giving equal structure along and across the wind.
    • Rationale: Isotropic turbulence's phase spectrum depends only on spatial frequency size.
    • User impact: CASA structure functions differ by a factor of five between the axes; casa-rs's are the same.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    settrop-simint-stamp: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: settrop with simint stamps a solution halfway to its interval's start
    • Inputs: settrop with simint longer than an integration (e.g. simint=6 on 2 s integrations).
    • CASA: setSimulate stamps each solution (t0+t)/2, so times are 0,1,2,6,7,8 s for integrations at 0,2,4,6,8,10 s, and corrupt gives later integrations a slot from earlier in the interval.
    • casa-rs: Stamps each solution with its integration's time, as CASA does when simint is unset.
    • Rationale: Each integration should use its own time.
    • User impact: Time-varying troposphere in later integrations of an interval lags in CASA.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-hcn-106ghz-line: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM's HCN catalogue misplaces a line at 106 GHz
    • Inputs: Any ATM calculation near 106.3 GHz.
    • CASA: mkSpecificRefractivity_hcn lists the J=12-11 line as 106.298069519 GHz (decimal point moved from 1062.98), so ATM sums an HCN line there and none at 1063 GHz; zenith opacity 0.029368 against 0.028919 corrected, 1.5% high.
    • casa-rs: Moves the decimal point back (1062.98069519 GHz), one of four corrections in atm-corrections.patch.
    • Rationale: The catalogue (JPL c027001.cat) puts the line at 1062.98 GHz; owner decision.
    • User impact: Opacity near 106 GHz is about 1.5% lower than CASA's; elsewhere changes are below 3.5e-5.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-24 (#841 Phase 4): ATM's data errors are corrected.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-air-density-sea-level: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM's reference air densities drop a digit at sea level
    • Inputs: ATM with mid-latitude, subarctic or US Standard atmospheres at sea level to 1 km.
    • CASA: st76's sea-level reference air densities lost their leading digit (.496E+19 etc., about 2.5e19 meant), so minor gases (O3, N2O, CO, NO2, SO2, HCl, HCN) are up to about four times too scarce in the lowest layers.
    • casa-rs: Restores the digit (2.496e19, 2.711e19, 2.549e19, 2.855e19, 2.548e19 cm^-3).
    • Rationale: Air density falls with height; US Standard gives 2.547e19 by the ideal gas law (owner decision).
    • User impact: Sites below 1 km see O3, CO, N2O line opacities up to 1.8%, 7.7%, 8.5% higher; sites at 1 km and above are unchanged.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-24 (#841 Phase 4): ATM's data errors are corrected.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-h2-17o-abundance: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM weights H2(17)O by the (18)O abundance
    • Inputs: Any ATM wet opacity calculation.
    • CASA: ratiohh17o uses the (18)O abundance (0.0020439) where the (17)O abundance (0.000375) belongs, so H2(17)O lines are 5.45 times too strong.
    • casa-rs: Uses abun_17o, as ATM does for oxygen and ozone.
    • Rationale: The siblings use abun_17o (owner decision).
    • User impact: Wet opacity falls by up to 0.61% (990 GHz); at Chajnantor 230 GHz opacity moves by 2e-4.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-24 (#841 Phase 4): ATM's data errors are corrected.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-hdo-weight: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM's HDO weight divides by three
    • Inputs: Any ATM wet opacity calculation.
    • CASA: ratiohdo reads 2-0abun_d for 2.0abun_d, so the denominator is 3.0024 instead of 1.0030 and HDO lines are about three times too weak.
    • casa-rs: Uses 2.0*abun_d.
    • Rationale: It is a typo; every sibling ratio uses 2.0*abun_d (owner decision).
    • User impact: At Chajnantor zenith opacity rises 0.32% at 230 GHz and 0.15% at 90 GHz; wet opacity up to 1.8%.
    • Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-24 (#841 Phase 4): ATM's data errors are corrected.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-hcn-profile-100km: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM's HCN profile stops at 100 km
    • Inputs: ATM profiles topped above about 90 km.
    • CASA: hcn[50] is initialised with 45 values, so the last five levels (100 to 120 km) are zero and interpolated HCN falls to zero over them.
    • casa-rs: Follows it: there are no values to restore, and air above 90 km is about 2e-6 of the column.
    • Rationale: No data exist to replace the zeros.
    • User impact: Negligible effect, only on profiles topped above about 90 km.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-profile-copy-bottom-pressures: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Copying an AtmProfile copies its top pressures as bottom pressures
    • Inputs: Copying an AtmProfile (via RefractiveIndexProfile or SkyStatus) then getLayerBottomPressure or setAltitude.
    • CASA: The copy constructor fills v_layerPressure0_ from a.v_layerPressure1_, so bottom pressures return the top pressures; no opacity, path or brightness calculation reads them.
    • casa-rs: Keeps one profile and never copies it through such a path.
    • Rationale: The defect cannot arise in what casa-rs ports.
    • User impact: No effect on results.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-ozone-isotopologue-padding: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM leaves the ozone isotopologue layers unpadded
    • Inputs: An ATM user profile with a layer that has zero ozone.
    • CASA: mkRefractiveIndexProfile pushes a zero onto the total-ozone vector only, so the eight ozone state and isotopologue vectors become shorter than the layer count and later layers' values shift down one place.
    • casa-rs: The document records no casa-rs action; ATM's standard profiles have ozone in every layer so it does not arise.
    • Rationale: Latent ATM bug that does not arise with ATM's own profiles.
    • User impact: Only user profiles with zero-ozone layers would be affected.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-omits-16o17o: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ATM omits (16)O(17)O
    • Inputs: ATM opacity near (16)O(17)O lines.
    • CASA: mkSpecificRefractivity_16o17o returns zero before its line loop; the comment says the isotope was removed in 2024 while its catalogue is reviewed because it gave lines that do not exist.
    • casa-rs: The port returns zero too and keeps the catalogue in its tables for the record.
    • Rationale: The omission is deliberate in ATM.
    • User impact: Results match ATM, without (16)O(17)O lines.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    atm-averaged-nondispersive-wet-phase: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: RefractiveIndexProfile's averaged non-dispersive wet phase reads a channel as a water column
    • Inputs: RefractiveIndexProfile::getAverageNonDispersiveH2OPhaseDelay on a window (e.g. 22 GHz, 1.3 mm water).
    • CASA: The call getNonDispersiveH2OPhaseDelay(v_transfertId_[spwid]+i) converts the index to a Length in metres, returning channel 0's phase for a water column of 0, 1, 2 ... m, giving 306,132 degrees; CASA's at tool (SkyStatus) is unaffected.
    • casa-rs: casa-atmosphere takes the mean of the channels' phases.
    • Rationale: The overload resolution is an error.
    • User impact: The window-averaged phase is a few degrees, not hundreds of thousands.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    mosaic-multiple-models-cache-geometry: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Mosaic multi-image prediction depends on image order
    • Inputs: sm.predict with two differently sized Stokes I images through the mosaic machine, in both orders, compared with independent single-image predictions.
    • CASA: CASA 6.7.6.14 joint predictions differ from the independent sum by 0.00353/0.0326 NRMS, and swapping the images changes DATA by 0.0307. The public convolution-cache key omits image geometry; repeated identical images accumulate correctly.
    • casa-rs: Prepares every image with its own geometry and sums the predictions, matching the independent CASA single-image sum to 2.11e-6 NRMS.
    • Rationale: SkyEquation intends to add every model image. Reusing an earlier image geometry corrupts DATA according to model order.
    • User impact: Multi-image mosaic prediction is linear across models; raw defective CASA joint outputs remain recorded and tested separately.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record, predict-multiple-models-20261111.
    flagged-component-duplication: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Mixed image/component prediction doubles components at flagged samples
    • Inputs: Existing MS with independent sample and row flags, predicted from an image and a 0.25 Jy component, replacing and incremental.
    • CASA: Retains the component in skipped flagged samples, then adds it again; joint flagged DATA contains two component contributions.
    • casa-rs: Adds the masked image prediction and each component once, using independent CASA image-only and component-only sums as the reference.
    • Rationale: The sky-model sum must not double components when an image is present; the upstream accumulator retains and adds the previous model at skipped samples.
    • User impact: Corrected flagged mixed-model DATA; the raw defective joint CASA DATA remains recorded and its excess is tested.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record, flagged-samples-20261112.
    invalid-leakage-solutions: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Invalid leakage solutions become unit leakage
    • Inputs: D calibration table with either receptor flagged or an antenna missing; nearest/linear time interpolation.
    • CASA: Substitutes both leakage coefficients with one, adding parallel hands to cross hands;3+4j becomes9+12j on an autocorrelation. Data flags remain unchanged.
    • casa-rs: Uses the neutral zero leakage pair for that invalid antenna, preserving valid antenna/time solutions. G retains CASA per-receptor unit substitution.
    • Rationale: One is neutral for diagonal gain but fabricates leakage in the linear D operator.
    • User impact: Invalid calibration cannot manufacture large unflagged cross-hand signals. Raw defective CASA results remain recorded and C++ table consumption reproduces them independently.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record, apply-flags-20261112.
    natural-spline-first-knot: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: Natural-spline interpolation skips the first interior back-substitution
    • Inputs: Frequency-dependent complex table, natural-spline interpolation of amplitudes/phases; four-point Tf closed-form discriminator.
    • CASA: Leaves the first interior second derivative partly eliminated, producing a derivative discontinuity; the discriminator differs by NRMS0.0780.
    • casa-rs: Completes back-substitution at every interior knot. The exact half-knot amplitudes are independently checked and supplied through a separately sampled CASA table.
    • Rationale: Natural cubic splines require continuous first derivatives at every interior knot; skipping one contradicts the stated mathematical method.
    • User impact: Corrected frequency-interpolated values; raw CASA outputs and its consumption of native tables remain separately verified. Numerical tolerances are unchanged.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record, apply-frequency-20261112.
