    simutil¶

    simutil method implementation¶

    Implementation status is not behavioral verification. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise NotImplementedError naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

    Audit baseline: 49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947. Status counts: Implemented: 46, Partial: 2, Not implemented: 9, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

    CASA method CASA parameters Implementation Scope Evidence
    applyRotate x, y, tcos, tsin Implemented Returns the rotated x and y coordinates using the input numeric types, including NumPy arrays. signature, arithmetic-casa
    approxBeam configfile, freq Implemented CASA's approximate synthesized beam of a configuration at a frequency. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    average_direction directions=None Implemented CASA's mean of directions, as text at its precision. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    bandname freq Implemented CASA's band names. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    baselineLengths configfile Implemented Baseline lengths of a configuration file, as CASA computes them. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    calc_pointings2 spacing, size, maptype='hex', direction=None, relmargin=0.5, beam=0.0 Implemented Hexagonal, square and ALMA tilings of a map about a direction or the object's own, CASA's pointing text exactly; kept as pointings. CASA's ALMA tiling without a beam raises; casa-rs uses simobserve's beam (decision below). signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    cellsize image Implemented The image's cell as two quantities in radians, positive; a rotated image raises CASA's message. signature, simanalyze-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    closereport Implemented Closes self.report; AttributeError when none was opened, as in CASA. signature, helpers-casa
    convimage modelflat, outflat, complist='' Partial (#1053) Regrids the flat model onto the flat image and convolves it with the image's beam, writing CASA's .regrid and .regrid.conv; a complist is refused (#1053). signature, simanalyze-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    dir_m2s dir Implemented Writes a direction measure as simutil's text. signature, binding-tests
    dir_s2m direction=None Implemented CASA's direction measure for a string (J2000 when it names no frame, degrees for bare numbers), a list's mean, or self.direction; the verbose J2000 message. signature, helpers-casa
    direction_splitter direction=None Implemented Splits a direction into epoch and two quantities; a list of one is split at its rounded mean, as CASA does. A direction without an epoch, which CASA cannot split, is refused (decision below). signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    endfig show=True, filename='' Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    ephemeris date, usehourangle=True, direction=None, telescope=None, ms=None, cofa=None Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    flatimage image, complist='', verbose=False Implemented Collapses a model to CASA's flat image, adding a component list as CASA does. signature, skymodel-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    getBaseTriangularTiling width, height, angle, spacing, pb Implemented Returns the two coordinate lists for the CASA triangular pointing pattern; zero spacing raises ZeroDivisionError. signature, tiling-casa
    getdatum datumcode, verbose=False Implemented CASA's datum table: (dx, dy, dz, er, rf), whole offsets as ints; an unknown datum raises CASA's message. signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    getShiftTriangularTiling width, height, angle, spacing, pb Implemented Returns the two coordinate lists for the CASA triangular pointing pattern; zero spacing raises ZeroDivisionError. signature, tiling-casa
    getTrianglePoints width, height, angle, spacing Implemented Returns the two coordinate lists for the CASA triangular pointing pattern; zero spacing raises ZeroDivisionError. signature, tiling-casa
    getTriangularTiling longlength, latlength, angle, spacing, pb Implemented Returns the two coordinate lists for the CASA triangular pointing pattern; zero spacing raises ZeroDivisionError. signature, tiling-casa
    imclean mstoimage, imagename, cleanmode, psfmode, cell, imsize, imcenter, interactive, niter, threshold, weighting, outertaper, pbcor, stokes, sourcefieldlist='', modelimage='', mask=[], dryrun=False Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    imtclean mstoimage, imagename, gridder, deconvolver, cell, imsize, imdirection, interactive, niter, threshold, weighting, outertaper, pbcor, stokes, modelimage='', mask=[], dryrun=False Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    is4d image Implemented Whether a CASA image or FITS file has direction, Stokes and spectral axes in CASA's order; casa-rs leaves no image open (recorded deviation). signature, helpers-casa
    isdirection s, halt=True Implemented CASA's direction test, with its message when halt. signature, binding-tests
    isEven num Implemented Tests parity using the input numeric type, including large integers and NumPy arrays. signature, arithmetic-casa
    ismstp s, halt=False Implemented Whether a MeasurementSet is total power: its first antenna's name holds TP, it has one antenna, or every UVW value is zero (CASA tests any; decision ismstp-any-zero-uvw). With halt, CASA's messages. signature, simanalyze-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    isquantity s, halt=True Implemented CASA's quantity test, with its message when halt. signature, binding-tests
    isreport Implemented Whether self.report is the file self.reportfile names; true after closereport, as in CASA. signature, helpers-casa
    itrf2loc x, y, z, cx, cy, cz Implemented ITRF to local east, north and up about a centre, as NumPy arrays. signature, binding-tests
    itrf2locname x, y, z, obsname Implemented itrf2loc about a known observatory's WGS84 position, as CASA finds it with me.observatory and long2xyz. signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    locxyz2itrf lat, longitude, alt, locx=0.0, locy=0.0, locz=0.0 Implemented Local east, north and up offsets about a WGS84 point to Earth-centred (x, y, z). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    long2xyz lon, lat, elevation, datum Implemented Geodetic longitude, latitude and elevation on a datum to Earth-centred (x, y, z). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    median_direction directions=None Implemented CASA's median of directions, as text at its precision. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    modifymodel inimage, outimage, inbright, indirection, incell, incenter, inwidth, innchan, flatimage=False Implemented Rewrites a sky model's brightness, direction, cell, centre and width as CASA does and returns CASA's tuple; the axis, Stokes and two-cell defects listed below are decisions. signature, skymodel-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    msg s, origin=None, priority=None Implemented Logs at the level CASA's log shows (INFO, WARNING, DEBUG) on casars.; priority='error' raises RuntimeError with the message; with verbose, writes the open report as CASA does and logs once at INFO (recorded deviation). signature, helpers-casa
    needsFiller length, spacing, bs, npoints Implemented Returns integer 1 if the map length exceeds the pattern plus beam, else 0. signature, arithmetic-casa
    newfig multi=0, show=True Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    nextfig Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    noisetemp telescope=None, freq=None, diam=None, epsilon=None, correlator=None Implemented CASA's receiver and atmosphere parameters for ALMA, ACA, total power, the (E)VLA, SMA and other telescopes; the band-edge lookups CASA gets wrong are decisions below. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    openreport Implemented Opens self.reportfile as self.report, a Python file object, appending to one that exists; CASA's refusal when reportfile is not set. signature, helpers-casa
    plotants x, y, z, d, name Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    polsettings telescope, poltype=None, listall=False Implemented CASA's feed and correlation settings per telescope. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    read_pointings filename Implemented Reads CASA's pointing files; the text read is CASA's. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    readantenna antab=None Implemented Reads CASA's antenna configuration files (XYZ, UTM, LOC and geodetic, with observatory and COFA headers) and returns CASA's tuple; keeps the telescope as telescopename. The two CASA reading bugs it does not follow are decisions below. signature, readantenna-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    sensitivity freq, bandwidth, etime, elevation, pwv=None, telescope=None, diam=None, nant=None, antennalist=None, doimnoise=None, integration=None, debug=None, method='tsys-atm', tau0=None, t_sky=None Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    setcfg mysm, telescope, x, y, z, diam, padnames, posobs, mounttype=None Implemented Calls the simulator's setconfig as CASA does (global coordinates, CASA's mount guess, diam.tolist()), returns the mount; a refused configuration closes the simulator and raises ValueError. signature, helpers-casa
    sfBeam1d beam='', cell='', convsupport=-1, sampling='' Not implemented (#1053) Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053, which classifies it. signature, refusals
    spectral image Implemented (nchan as a one-element int64 array, width as CASA's string) or 0.0 without a spectral axis, for CASA images and FITS files. signature, helpers-casa
    statim image, plot=True, incell=None, disprange=None, bar=True, showstats=True Partial (#1025) CASA's (min, max, rms, unit), the values 0-d float64 arrays as CASA returns them. plot=True, CASA's default, draws no figure yet and says so (#1025). A Jy/beam image without a beam is not scaled by the pixel area as CASA scales it (recorded deviation). signature, simanalyze-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    tconpc sf, orlim, er, esq, rf Implemented The transverse Mercator zone constants (eps, r, so, v0, v2, v4, v6). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    tmgeod n, e, eps, cm, fe, sf, so, r, v0, v2, v4, v6, fn, er, esq Implemented Grid coordinates to (lat, lon, conv, kp), the longitude west-positive. signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    utm2long east, north, zone, datum, nors Implemented UTM easting and northing to (lon, lat) on a datum; verbose's position message is not logged. signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    utm2xyz easting, northing, elevation, zone, datum, nors Implemented UTM easting, northing and elevation to Earth-centred (x, y, z). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    wrapang ang, target, period=360.0 Implemented CASA's angle wrapping. signature, simutil-casa-fixture
    write_pointings filename, pointings, time=1.0 Implemented Writes CASA's pointing file text. signature, simutil-casa-fixture, binding-tests
    xyz2long x, y, z, datum Implemented Earth-centred x, y, z to (lon, lat, height) on a datum; CASA subtracts the x offset from y, casa-rs the y offset (decision xyz2long-y-offset). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests
    xyz2long_old x, y, z, datum Implemented The older iteration, (lon, lat); CASA subtracts the x offset from z, casa-rs the z offset (decision xyz2long-old-z-offset). signature, geodesy-casa, binding-tests

    simutil interacting modes and tool behavior¶

    simutil deviations and unfinished work¶

    readantenna-stale-loop-variable: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simutil.readantenna averages a stale loop variable
    • Inputs: readantenna on a configuration file with absolute coordinates, no known observatory and no COFA header.
    • CASA: readantenna averages the UTM branch's loop variables x, y, z instead of stnx, stny, stnz: UnboundLocalError for an XYZ file, the last antenna for a UTM file; it also swaps latitude and longitude from xyz2long.
    • casa-rs: Converts the mean antenna position, as the code's comment intends (casa_simulation::readantenna).
    • Rationale: The comment shows the observatory was meant to sit at the mean antenna position.
    • User impact: Such configuration files work in casa-rs instead of raising or placing the observatory wrongly.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    readantenna-blank-line-names: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simutil.readantenna names antennas after blank lines
    • Inputs: readantenna on a configuration file with blank lines, e.g. CASA's own WSRT.cfg (two blank lines).
    • CASA: A line with fewer than four values is skipped but still gets a default pad and antenna name, so readantenna returns 16 names for 14 WSRT antennas starting A00, A01, RT0.
    • casa-rs: Skips such lines entirely.
    • Rationale: Names must stay aligned with positions.
    • User impact: CASA misnames every dish in a WSRT simulation; casa-rs does not.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    calc-pointings2-undefined-pbcoeff: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: calc_pointings2's ALMA tiling reads an undefined pbcoeff
    • Inputs: calc_pointings2 with maptype='ALMA' (or alma, ALMA-OT) and no primary beam (beam <= 0), e.g. simobserve with an ALMA map and no antenna list.
    • CASA: The default spacing_asecpbcoeffsqrt(3) uses pbcoeff, a local of task_simobserve.py not defined in simutil.py, so the call raises NameError (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Uses simobserve's value 1.13 (PBCOEFF), so the beam is spacing x 1.13 x sqrt(3).
    • Rationale: The default clearly intends simobserve's value.
    • User impact: The call works instead of raising NameError.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    noisetemp-table-edges: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: noisetemp reads the wrong receiver temperature at the edges of its tables
    • Inputs: noisetemp at exactly the first band edge (e.g. ALMA at 35 GHz, VLA 0.8-1 GHz, SMA 180 GHz) or above 900 GHz for an unknown telescope such as WSRT.
    • CASA: The lookup leaves z=0 at the first edge so t0[-1] (the last entry) is used (230 K instead of 28 K for ALMA at 35 GHz), and for unknown telescopes the test reads f0[2] before checking z and raises IndexError (CASA 6.7.6, WSRT at 950 GHz).
    • casa-rs: Takes the first temperature at the first edge and keeps the last temperature (200 K) above the last edge.
    • Rationale: The lookup was meant to return the band's own receiver temperature.
    • User impact: Receiver temperatures at band edges are right instead of wrapping around, and no IndexError.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    direction-splitter-no-epoch: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: direction_splitter cannot split a direction without an epoch
    • Inputs: direction_splitter on a direction without an epoch, e.g. '18h00m00 -23d00m00'.
    • CASA: It reads the longitude from field 1 and the latitude from field 2 whatever the count, so a direction without an epoch raises IndexError and an unmatched latitude raises AttributeError (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Refuses both with "can't interpret ... as a direction" (the message isdirection gives); more than three fields split as CASA splits them.
    • Rationale: A clear error is better than a traceback.
    • User impact: simobserve stops with a clear message instead of a traceback.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    modifymodel-axis-swap: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: modifymodel swaps axes into place and refuses a cyclic order
    • Inputs: modifymodel with a model whose axes are a cyclic order, e.g. [spectral, RA, Dec, Stokes].
    • CASA: The swapaxes loop orders only transpositions (e.g. the FITS order), leaving a cyclic order out of place so the check raises "internal error: I'm confused about the shape if your model data cube" with the new image already created and empty (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Permutes the axes, so any order of the four is written as [RA, Dec, Stokes, spectral].
    • Rationale: A model's axis order should not matter.
    • User impact: Models in any axis order work; CASA refuses cyclic orders.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    modifymodel-stokes-labels: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: modifymodel labels the new image's Stokes planes by their count
    • Inputs: modifymodel on a model labelled I and V, or on three unlabelled planes.
    • CASA: The written image takes its Stokes axis from ia.fromshape's default, labelling planes by count (I, IQ, IQU, IQUV), so I and V comes out I and Q and the V data are read as Stokes Q; three unlabelled planes come out IQU though modifymodel returns 'IQV'.
    • casa-rs: Keeps the model's labels and labels unlabelled planes as modifymodel reports them; circular or linear bases keep their labels, so flatimage and adding components refuse them.
    • Rationale: modifymodel itself returns the model's Stokes labels.
    • User impact: CASA mislabels non-I Stokes planes; casa-rs preserves them but refuses polarisation bases CASA relabels.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    modifymodel-incell-map: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: modifymodel indexes the map it makes of two cells
    • Inputs: modifymodel with incell given as a list of two cells.
    • CASA: incell = map(qa.convert, incell, ['arcsec','arcsec']) is then indexed as incell[0]; under Python 3 map returns an iterator, so it raises TypeError: 'map' object is not subscriptable (CASA 6.7.6).
    • casa-rs: Takes the two cells, converted to arcsec and used as given (signs included), as under Python 2; a single cell is unaffected.
    • Rationale: The code was written for Python 2.
    • User impact: A two-cell incell works instead of raising TypeError.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    ia-modify-default-measures-frame: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: ia.modify converts a component spectrum through the image's default measures frame
    • Inputs: ia.modify adding a component list to an image from ia.fromshape (as modifymodel and simobserve make), with spectra in a different frame, e.g. TOPO spectrum on an LSRK image.
    • CASA: Each spectral model converts its reference frequency through the image's default measures frame (geocentre, epoch 0, J2000 pole), shifting frequencies by a velocity no observation has, about 1e-4 of the frequency in CASA 6.7.6.
    • casa-rs: Converts when the image's conversion layer carries a real frame (epoch and observatory position) and otherwise takes the frequencies as they are.
    • Rationale: A meaningful conversion needs the observation's epoch and position, which such a sky model does not carry.
    • User impact: Frequencies and spectral-index fluxes differ from CASA by about 1e-4 on frame-mismatched components; matched frames agree to 1e-6.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    ismstp-any-zero-uvw: Previously agreed¶
    • Record: simutil.ismstp takes any zero UVW value for total power
    • Inputs: simobserve corrupting an existing MeasurementSet (obsmode='') whose first antenna name lacks 'TP' and which has more than one antenna.
    • CASA: The test uvw.all() == 0. is true when any UVW value is zero, not all, so an interferometer MeasurementSet with one zero coordinate is corrupted as single dish with wrong file names and sensitivity constant.
    • casa-rs: Tests whether every value is zero.
    • Rationale: The test is meant to detect total power data.
    • User impact: Interferometric data with one zero UVW value are handled as interferometry.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: bugs-record.
    xyz2long-y-offset: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: xyz2long on a datum whose x and y offsets differ, such as NAD27.
    • CASA: Computes the longitude as atan2(y-dx, x-dx), subtracting the x offset from y; long2xyz then xyz2long on NAD27 at ALMA moves the longitude by 2.2 arcseconds.
    • casa-rs: Subtracts dy, so xyz2long inverts long2xyz.
    • Rationale: The rest of the function subtracts (dx, dy, dz); recorded in docs/CASA (C++) bugs.md as "simutil.xyz2long subtracts the x offset from y".
    • User impact: Longitudes on datums other than WGS 84 and NAD83 are correct; WGS 84's agree with CASA's.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: geodesy-casa.
    xyz2long-old-z-offset: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: xyz2long_old on a datum whose x and z offsets differ.
    • CASA: Its second term is (z-dx)2/er2, subtracting the x offset from z.
    • casa-rs: Subtracts dz.
    • Rationale: The rest of the function subtracts (dx, dy, dz); recorded in docs/CASA (C++) bugs.md as "simutil.xyz2long_old subtracts the x offset from z".
    • User impact: Latitudes on such datums are slightly different from CASA's; longitudes and WGS 84's results agree.
    • Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
    • Evidence: geodesy-casa.
    msg-verbose-single-post: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: simutil(verbose=True).msg(s, origin=..., priority=...)
    • CASA: Posts the message twice to its log: once with ANSI colour codes around [origin], WARNING and ERROR, once with WARNING and ERROR coloured; both at INFO.
    • casa-rs: Logs it once at INFO on casars., without colour codes; the report file is written as CASA writes it.
    • Rationale: Colour codes in a log record are terminal noise, and Python logging shows the message once to every handler.
    • User impact: A script that counts or parses CASA's duplicated, coloured lines sees one plain line.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: helpers-casa.
    is4d-leaves-image-open: Previously agreed¶
    • Inputs: simutil.is4d on an image that is not CASA's 4-D order.
    • CASA: Returns False before ia.done(), leaving the shared image tool open; the next ia.open warns 'Another image is already open, closing first'.
    • casa-rs: Opens nothing that outlives the call; no warning follows.
    • Rationale: The warning reports CASA's leaked state, not anything about the image.
    • User impact: None on results.
    • Owner decision: Owner approved on 2026-11-05; see docs/simulation/owner-decisions-20261105.md.
    • Evidence: helpers-casa.

    simutil evidence¶

    Evidence is scoped to its named revision, inputs and comparator. Historical reported results need applicability review; planned or unexecuted tests are not passes.

    geodesy-casa¶

    CASA-recorded values — measured. Recorded from CASA 6.7.6-14 by simutil_geodesy_oracle.py: getdatum of all 24 datums and an unknown one, tconpc and tmgeod of three ellipsoids and five UTM points, the UTM and geodetic conversions on four datums, locxyz2itrf about three points and itrf2locname about five observatories. The Python tests agree to 1e-12 (positions to 1e-6 m), and where CASA slips on a datum's offsets casa-rs inverts long2xyz instead. The geodesy helpers.

    • crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_simutil.py

    helpers-casa¶

    CASA-recorded values — measured. Recorded from CASA 6.7.6-14 by simutil_helpers_oracle.py: is4d and spectral on the 22 sky-model input images, dir_s2m on 13 directions, msg at 9 priorities with and without verbose and an origin, the report helpers through a task's sequence and the report file they write, and setcfg on 8 telescopes with a stand-in simulator. The Python tests agree on every return value, raised error, log level and the report's text, and on the arguments setcfg passes. msg, isreport, openreport, closereport, dir_s2m, is4d, spectral and setcfg.

    • crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_simutil.py

    tiling-casa¶

    arithmetic-casa¶

    CASA-recorded values — measured. Nineteen cases recorded from CASA 6.7.6.14 cover scalars, large integers, NumPy arrays and scalars, boundary values and errors; the Python binding agrees on values and result types. applyRotate, isEven and needsFiller.
